MongoDB silently skips documents that lack the indexed field or whose field isn't a date — no error, no warning, no log. A snake_case-vs-camelCase typo between bson:"expiresAt" and a Keys:{"expires_at"} index leaks storage forever and you find out weeks later via the cost graph. - **monochrome-manga-color**: Wibi Style 官方视觉风格：把一张照片转换成黑白漫画纯色背景。适合动作、神态清楚的单人日常照片，转为黑白漫画人物和简化彩色场景。适用于用户要求黑白漫画纯色背景，或明确调用 $monochrome-manga-color 时。 ([Vieeeeeee/wibi-style](https://github.com/Vieeeeeee/wibi-style/tree/HEAD/skills/monochrome-manga-color) · ★153)

Best for understanding dynamic biological processes from scRNA-seq data.
